# Armoured Souls — Expansion Robot Blueprints

**Status:** complete design draft for review  
**Updated:** 9 October 2026  
**Coverage:** all 89 proposed stables; 326 slotted robot blueprints and 7 fully specified alternatives (333 robot designs).

Every named robot from the expansion proposal is covered, including reserves. Previously open-ended machine classes now have finite named slots. Source aliases distinguish familiar characters from unique catalogue names.

## Rules used

This companion uses the newer starter catalogue found with the completed starter portrait pack. It supersedes the initial pasted draft on fixed team membership, stable-wide event orders, Swarm size, weapon progression, and image handling. The original eleven stables and their portrait files are unchanged.

- Each main roster has at most 11 continuous slots. Swarms open with 3 and target 11; Paragons open/target 1; Duos open/target 2; Trios open/target 3; Tycoons and Generalists open with 2 and target 3.
- Tycoon and Generalist use explicit robot-target overrides of 3. Other strategy targets follow the newer starter defaults. Reserve activation stays within the documented strategy maximum.
- Teams follow fixed slot pairing. Every possible team supported by the proposed roster has a name, including teams that form only after reserve activation.
- Every stable has all nine event types exactly once. Event order belongs to the stable; it is not duplicated as a conflicting preference list on each robot.
- Every robot has exactly two complete weapon stages. Final weapons follow the newer starter hand/range maxima. Prices and compatibility are copied from the supplied references.
- Refinements use verified weapon/attribute combinations from the starter examples. Swarm, Trio, and Tycoon have explicit empty plans. Empty plans for other weapons identify the reason. No weapon attribute was guessed.
- Range is informational and derived from the weapons. Images are separate assets found by slot convention; no images are generated by this blueprint update.
- ATLAS, Atlas AS7-D, and Atlas Tenma are distinct names. Movie Ratchet is Phineas Ratchet to avoid the existing Autobot. A.I. David and Alien David are David Mecha and David 8.

## Strategy capacity and alternatives

The first concept list included more characters than some strategies support. Those characters remain fully designed as authoring alternatives: Northstar replaces Titan slot 4; Ramattra replaces Omnic slot 4; Phineas Ratchet replaces Robot City slot 3; Foxy replaces Fazbear slot 3; Black Lion replaces Voltron slot 4; Platinum or Lead replaces Metal Men slot 4. Only one blueprint occupies a slot in an approved runtime catalogue. Alternative portraits use that slot path only if the alternative is selected.

Open-ended reserves are resolved as Lewa for the Toa; SAR for Lost in Space; Fix-It Scout 03 for the Fix-It machines; and the full remaining Metal Men as alternatives. All nine Team Fortress robot classes are included, plus two giant variants. Getter Robo uses one Getter-1 portrait, with no separate robot slots for its alternate forms.

## Validation boundary

The review JSON is fully expanded authoring data, not a claimed match for the unprovided backend schema. Local checks verify coverage, names, slots, strategy sizes, equipment compatibility, ranges, prices, team formation, event orders, and refinements against the supplied starter facts. STRATEGIES.md, the live weapon database, starting-credit budgets, profanity validation, and the game catalogue importer are not present here. Their checks remain necessary before importing; none of the blueprint fields below is deferred.

Global source rules: [newer starter catalogue](</Users/robertteunissen/.codex/attachments/f477bd41-0748-4ed0-950f-1e5d4e946df1/Pasted text.txt>) and [initial starter catalogue](</Users/robertteunissen/.codex/attachments/8ebcee69-f90e-4acb-8183-57d48fbe4692/Pasted text.txt>). Machine-specific identities come from the [expansion proposal](AUTOMATON_STABLE_EXPANSION.md).

**Full authoring record:** [moshimo-robotics.json](automaton-blueprints/moshimo-robotics.json).

---

## Shared art requirements

Each selected slot needs its own 512×512 WebP portrait under `app/frontend/public/assets/automatons/{catalogueKey}/{slot}.webp`. Use a dark industrial background, three-quarter view, consistent upper-right rim lighting, readable silhouettes, and source-specific identity and colours. The individual briefs above are complete descriptions, not a request to generate images during this update.

Game equipment is an arena interpretation. Recognizable fists, integrated emitters, tools, and iconic source weapons may supply the portrait silhouette while the blueprint uses the game catalogue equipment named above. Combining, flight, hacking, resurrection, and healing are not added as engine mechanics.
